height: auto; max-height: 55px;; aspect-ratio: 250 / 55; background-color: var(--bgColor-muted); border-radius: 6px\" class=\"js-gh-image-fallback\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/docs/screenshots/architecture.png\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/raw/main/docs/screenshots/thumbs/architecture.webp\" alt=\"Content site architecture\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/docs/screenshots/loop.png\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/raw/main/docs/screenshots/thumbs/loop.webp\" alt=\"The self-improving loop\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eNew in 2.0 — the Loop: flywheels with a shared-memory hub. The dashed lines are the write-backs.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eNew in 2.3: semantic system patterns and optional accessible motion, while static output stays the default.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eNew in 2.5.10: ten more layout grammars — Sankey, fishbone, Wardley map, kanban, user journey, deployment, dependency graph, UML class, story map, and database schema.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEditorial diagram types for Claude Code, Codex, Factory Droid, Pi, and Agent Skills-compatible hosts. Self-contained HTML + SVG. No shadows. No Mermaid slop. Semantic patterns describe behavior separately from layout, so a queue, policy trace, or trust boundary can use the nearest existing type without expanding the type count. Static HTML remains the default; optional motion is available for ordered explanations. The skill also redraws draw.io, Mermaid, or Excalidraw sources at a chosen format, size, and detail level.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNo Figma. No generic rounded boxes. No 30-minute color-picking sessions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhy I built it\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-why-i-built-it\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why I built it\" href=\"#why-i-built-it\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eI write at \u003ca href=\"https://littlemight.com?utm_source=diagram-design\u0026amp;utm_medium=readme\u0026amp;utm_campaign=github\u0026amp;utm_content=intro\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003elittlemight.com\u003c/a\u003e (and run \u003ca href=\"https://bestself.co?utm_source=diagram-design\u0026amp;utm_medium=readme\u0026amp;utm_campaign=github\u0026amp;utm_content=intro\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBestSelf.co\u003c/a\u003e on the side). Every time I needed a diagram — an architecture sketch, a flowchart, a pyramid of what matters most — I'd ask Claude and get back a generic rounded-box thing that looked nothing like the rest of the site. I'd either fight with Figma for 30 minutes or just skip the diagram.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSo I built a Claude Code skill for it. Editorial-quality visual types, matched to your brand in 60 seconds by reading your website.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eThe highest-quality move is usually deletion.\u003c/em\u003e Every node earns its place. The accent color is reserved for the 1–2 things the reader should look at first. Target density: 4/10.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat it makes\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-it-makes\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What it makes\" href=\"#what-it-makes\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery visual type ships in three static variants: minimal light, minimal dark, and full-editorial. Open any of them directly in a browser. Attribute-only comparisons remain tables; a single snapshot uses Architecture.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe v2.5.10 release added ten layout grammars. Compare their light, dark, and full-editorial variants in the \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/.github/pr-previews/editorial-diagrams-2.5.10.jpg\"\u003e30-variant contact sheet\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBrowse the live gallery:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ca href=\"https://cathrynlavery.github.io/diagram-design/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ecathrynlavery.github.io/diagram-design\u003c/a\u003e — or open \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/assets/index.html\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/assets/index.html\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e locally to flip through every diagram with light / dark / full-editorial tabs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstall\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-install\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Install\" href=\"#install\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOfficial builds come only from this repository. LittleMight, Cathryn Lavery's company, publishes the plugin directory listings; a listing under any other name is an unofficial copy. \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/PRIVACY.md\"\u003ePRIVACY.md\u003c/a\u003e lists what the skill sends over the network.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Code:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\n/plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\n/plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen enable updates once: run \u003ccode\u003e/plugin\u003c/code\u003e, open \u003cstrong\u003eMarketplaces\u003c/strong\u003e, select \u003cstrong\u003ediagram-design\u003c/strong\u003e, and choose \u003cstrong\u003eEnable auto-update\u003c/strong\u003e. Claude Code disables auto-update by default for third-party marketplaces; after this toggle, it refreshes the marketplace and installed plugin in the background after startup. Run \u003ccode\u003e/reload-plugins\u003c/code\u003e when prompted, or let the next session load the update.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodex:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"codex plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ncodex plugin add diagram-design@diagram-design\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecodex plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ncodex plugin add diagram-design@diagram-design\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex refreshes configured Git marketplaces at startup. To fetch immediately, run \u003ccode\u003ecodex plugin marketplace upgrade diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e and start a new session.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGitHub Copilot:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"copilot plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ncopilot plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecopilot plugin marketplace add cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ncopilot plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCopilot installs the shared Diagram Design skill plus its doctor, export, import, and profile capabilities from the existing repository marketplace. Confirm discovery with \u003ccode\u003ecopilot skill list\u003c/code\u003e (or \u003ccode\u003e/skills\u003c/code\u003e in an interactive session), then ask for a diagram in natural language. To fetch a merged update, run \u003ccode\u003ecopilot plugin marketplace update diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e, then \u003ccode\u003ecopilot plugin update diagram-design@diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFactory Droid:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"droid plugin marketplace add https://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ndroid plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design --scope user\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003edroid plugin marketplace add https://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design\ndroid plugin install diagram-design@diagram-design --scope user\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDroid tracks Git plugins by commit rather than the manifest's display version. To fetch a merged update, run \u003ccode\u003edroid plugin marketplace update diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e, then \u003ccode\u003edroid plugin update diagram-design@diagram-design --scope user\u003c/code\u003e, and start a new session.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Cowork (organization marketplace):\u003c/strong\u003e Organization GitHub marketplaces currently require a private or internal repository, so first mirror this public repository into one owned by your organization. In \u003cstrong\u003eOrganization settings → Plugins\u003c/strong\u003e, choose \u003cstrong\u003eAdd plugin → GitHub\u003c/strong\u003e, connect that mirror, and enable \u003cstrong\u003eSync automatically\u003c/strong\u003e from the marketplace menu. Automatic sync runs when a pull request containing a plugin version bump is merged to the mirror's default branch; direct pushes do not trigger the webhook. Install Diagram Design from the resulting organization marketplace.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePi:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"pi install https://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epi install https://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003e/reload\u003c/code\u003e in an open Pi session. Pi makes the skill available for matching diagram requests; use \u003ccode\u003e/skill:diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e to invoke it explicitly. Pi also loads the \u003ccode\u003e/export-diagram\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e/import-mermaid\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e/import-excalidraw\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e/profile\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e/doctor\u003c/code\u003e prompt templates. The unpinned Git install is intentional: Pi has no automatic package refresh, so run \u003ccode\u003epi update --extensions\u003c/code\u003e to pull merged updates.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKiro:\u003c/strong\u003e Import the Agent Skill from the repository subdirectory URL:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"https://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/tree/main/skills/diagram-design\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttps://github.com/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/tree/main/skills/diagram-design\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eKiro copies imported skills into \u003ccode\u003e.kiro/skills/\u003c/code\u003e for a workspace or \u003ccode\u003e~/.kiro/skills/\u003c/code\u003e globally, so re-import the URL to pick up updates. Custom agents that declare resources should include \u003ccode\u003eskill://diagram-design/**/SKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOpenCode:\u003c/strong\u003e Copy or symlink \u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003e.opencode/skills/diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e in a project or \u003ccode\u003e~/.config/opencode/skills/diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e globally. OpenCode has no Diagram Design marketplace package; copied installs update only when you replace the directory from a newer checkout.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOne-time migration:\u003c/strong\u003e an existing standalone \u003ccode\u003enpx skills add\u003c/code\u003e copy will not start following the Codex marketplace automatically. Remove that standalone copy, then use the Codex marketplace commands above. Likewise, uninstall a personal Cowork copy and reinstall Diagram Design from your organization's marketplace. Future marketplace version bumps then flow through each client's native update path.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eEditable install\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-editable-install\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Editable install\" href=\"#editable-install\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eManaged installs are convenient, but changes to \u003ccode\u003ereferences/style-guide.md\u003c/code\u003e may be replaced by package updates. Saved profiles in \u003ccode\u003e~/.diagram-design/profiles/\u003c/code\u003e survive updates, and projects with a \u003ccode\u003e.diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e marker are unaffected. Clone the repo and install the local path if you plan to customize the working style guide directly:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative \u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/\u003c/code\u003e. Pi discovers it through the repo's standard \u003ccode\u003eskills/\u003c/code\u003e package directory; Claude Code, GitHub Copilot, Codex, Factory Droid, and other Agent Skills-compatible tools use the same files.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eOnboarding — make it look like \u003cem\u003eyour\u003c/em\u003e brand\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-onboarding--make-it-look-like-your-brand\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Onboarding — make it look like your brand\" href=\"#onboarding--make-it-look-like-your-brand\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe whole point: ship editorial-quality diagrams in \u003cstrong\u003eyour\u003c/strong\u003e colors and typography, not a generic template.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOut of the box, diagrams render in a clean \u003cstrong\u003ejet-black + atomic-tangerine\u003c/strong\u003e palette (white-smoke paper, jet-black ink, atomic-tangerine accent, blue-slate muted, silver hairlines). Good enough to screenshot straight away. But 60 seconds of onboarding is better — the skill will pull your brand from your website and apply it across every diagram.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"You:     \u0026quot;onboard diagram-design to https://yoursite.com\u0026quot;\nAgent:   → fetches the homepage\n         → extracts the dominant palette + font stack\n         → maps detected values to semantic roles:\n             paper, ink, muted, accent, link\n         → shows a proposed diff\n         → writes your tokens to references/style-guide.md\nYou:     \u0026quot;yes, apply it\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eYou:     \"onboard diagram-design to https://yoursite.com\"\nAgent:   → fetches the homepage\n         → extracts the dominant palette + font stack\n         → maps detected values to semantic roles:\n             paper, ink, muted, accent, link\n         → shows a proposed diff\n         → writes your tokens to references/style-guide.md\nYou:     \"yes, apply it\"\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery new diagram now uses your colors. Your website's paper color becomes the diagram background. Your CTA color becomes the focal accent. Your body font stack becomes the node label family.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBrand matching also emits a fidelity receipt: sampled URLs, exact color roles, font families and weights, font source URLs, and any fallback. Public site fonts are used directly and verified after rendering rather than silently replaced with generic system fonts.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 \u003ccode\u003eink\u003c/code\u003e over \u003ccode\u003epaper\u003c/code\u003e. If your site has a color that fails contrast at diagram sizes (9–12px), it proposes an adjusted value and explains why.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eAccessible by default\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-accessible-by-default\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Accessible by default\" href=\"#accessible-by-default\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery diagram template gives the inline SVG an accessible name and description: \u003ccode\u003erole=\"img\"\u003c/code\u003e, a resolving \u003ccode\u003earia-labelledby\u003c/code\u003e, and first-child \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;title\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;desc\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e slots. IDs are prefixed per diagram and variant, so multiple SVG exports can be safely inlined on one page without duplicate accessible-name IDs. Decorative specimen icons are hidden from assistive technology instead.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eManual override\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-manual-override\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Manual override\" href=\"#manual-override\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrefer to set tokens by hand? Open \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/style-guide.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/style-guide.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e and edit the table. Everything downstream reads from there — every diagram, the annotation primitive, and the gallery all inherit semantic role names (\u003ccode\u003eaccent\u003c/code\u003e, not \u003ccode\u003e#eb6c36\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFirst-run gate\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-first-run-gate\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: First-run gate\" href=\"#first-run-gate\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe skill won't silently ship default-skinned diagrams into a branded project. On first use in a new project, it checks if \u003ccode\u003estyle-guide.md\u003c/code\u003e has been customized. If not, it pauses and asks:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003e\"This is your first diagram in this project. The style guide is still at the default. Want to run onboarding, paste tokens manually, or proceed with default?\"\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/onboarding.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/onboarding.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e for the full spec.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWorking with multiple clients\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-working-with-multiple-clients\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Working with multiple clients\" href=\"#working-with-multiple-clients\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOnboard a brand once, save the result as a named profile, then add a \u003ccode\u003e.diagram-design\u003c/code\u003e marker containing \u003ccode\u003eprofile: \u0026lt;slug\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e to each client project. Marker projects read \u003ccode\u003e~/.diagram-design/profiles/\u0026lt;slug\u0026gt;.md\u003c/code\u003e directly, so parallel workspaces can use different brands without overwriting a shared installed \u003ccode\u003estyle-guide.md\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe profile library is shared across Claude Code, Codex, Factory Droid, and Pi. Use \u003ccode\u003e/diagram-design:profile\u003c/code\u003e in Claude Code, \u003ccode\u003e/profile\u003c/code\u003e in Factory Droid or Pi, or ask in natural language in any host. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/profiles.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprofiles.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e for the storage, marker, and recovery contract.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuickstart\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-quickstart\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quickstart\" href=\"#quickstart\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# From a cloned checkout, open the gallery to see every diagram\nopen skills/diagram-design/assets/index.html       # macOS\nxdg-open skills/diagram-design/assets/index.html  # Linux\n\n# In Claude Code, Codex, Factory Droid, or Pi, ask:\n# \u0026quot;Make me an architecture diagram of my app: frontend, backend, database, Redis cache.\u0026quot;\n# \u0026quot;I need a quadrant showing Q2 projects by impact vs effort.\u0026quot;\n# \u0026quot;Give me a sequence of a bearer call with token refresh on 401.\u0026quot;\n# (branching refresh uses the ALT combined-fragment grammar in type-sequence.md;\n#  see skills/diagram-design/assets/example-sequence-oauth.html — not a full authorize-code handshake)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e From a cloned checkout, open the gallery to see every diagram\u003c/span\u003e\nopen skills/diagram-design/assets/index.html       \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e macOS\u003c/span\u003e\nxdg-open skills/diagram-design/assets/index.html  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Linux\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e In Claude Code, Codex, Factory Droid, or Pi, ask:\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e \"Make me an architecture diagram of my app: frontend, backend, database, Redis cache.\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e \"I need a quadrant showing Q2 projects by impact vs effort.\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e \"Give me a sequence of a bearer call with token refresh on 401.\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e (branching refresh uses the ALT combined-fragment grammar in type-sequence.md;\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e  see skills/diagram-design/assets/example-sequence-oauth.html — not a full authorize-code handshake)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOperator recipes for editable installs, first diagrams, brand setup, import, export, validation, Windows junctions, and reusable prompts live in \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/docs/cookbook.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003edocs/cookbook.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYour agent will pick the right type, build the HTML, and save it. You can also start from a template directly:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"cp skills/diagram-design/assets/template.html my-diagram.html        # minimal light\ncp skills/diagram-design/assets/template-full.html my-diagram.html   # editorial with summary cards\ncp skills/diagram-design/assets/template-motion.html my-diagram.html # optional accessible motion\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecp skills/diagram-design/assets/template.html my-diagram.html        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e minimal light\u003c/span\u003e\ncp skills/diagram-design/assets/template-full.html my-diagram.html   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e editorial with summary cards\u003c/span\u003e\ncp skills/diagram-design/assets/template-motion.html my-diagram.html \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e optional accessible motion\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSemantic patterns and optional motion\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-semantic-patterns-and-optional-motion\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Semantic patterns and optional motion\" href=\"#semantic-patterns-and-optional-motion\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen behavior matters, the skill chooses a semantic pattern first and a visual type second. The nine routed patterns cover fan-in queues and bottlenecks, repeated stage slots, unstructured-input transformation, paired policy traces, secure paved roads, governance catalogs, compensating security layers, traceable block decomposition, and lifecycle phase maps. Each pattern defines its triggers, primitives, budget, anti-patterns, static fallback, and nearest visual type in \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/semantic-patterns.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003esemantic-patterns.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMotion is optional and does not create another visual type. \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/animation.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eanimation.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e defines \u003ccode\u003enone\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ereveal\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estep\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eloop\u003c/code\u003e modes with a complete static first frame, deterministic timing, and controls when interaction is available. Reduced-motion output shows the complete static frame and hides/disables playback controls. Motion HTML uses the exact reviewed controller from \u003ccode\u003etemplate-motion.html\u003c/code\u003e; arbitrary or modified inline scripts, remote assets, CSS imports, and executable HTML attributes are rejected. The default is \u003ccode\u003enone\u003c/code\u003e: ordinary output remains static and script-free. \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/assets/example-policy-trace-animated.html\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eexample-policy-trace-animated.html\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e is the self-contained interactive example.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eImport from draw.io, Mermaid, or Excalidraw\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-import-from-drawio-mermaid-or-excalidraw\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Import from draw.io, Mermaid, or Excalidraw\" href=\"#import-from-drawio-mermaid-or-excalidraw\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAlready have diagrams in draw.io / diagrams.net, Mermaid, or Excalidraw? Point the skill at the source and it \u003cstrong\u003eredraws\u003c/strong\u003e them — same content, this design system, at whatever the destination needs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/docs/screenshots/import-drawio.png\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/raw/main/docs/screenshots/thumbs/import-drawio.webp\" alt=\"Redrawn from a .drawio file\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eA 12-node draw.io file redrawn at \u003ccode\u003ebalanced\u003c/code\u003e detail for a blog post. The source's six pastel fills became one accent; its hand-dragged coordinates became a 4px grid.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio\n/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified --audience=executive\n/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio --detail=faithful --format=png --page=all\n/diagram-design:import-mermaid README.md --diagram=all\n/diagram-design:import-mermaid architecture.mmd --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified\n/diagram-design:import-excalidraw whiteboard.excalidraw --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio\n/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified --audience=executive\n/diagram-design:import-drawio platform.drawio --detail=faithful --format=png --page=all\n/diagram-design:import-mermaid README.md --diagram=all\n/diagram-design:import-mermaid architecture.mmd --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified\n/diagram-design:import-excalidraw whiteboard.excalidraw --size=slide-16x9 --detail=simplified\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr just ask: \u003cem\u003e\"redraw this drawio file for my deck\"\u003c/em\u003e, \u003cem\u003e\"make this Mermaid block editorial\"\u003c/em\u003e, \u003cem\u003e\"make this whiteboard sketch presentable\"\u003c/em\u003e, or \u003cem\u003e\"この Mermaid をスライド用にきれいにして\"\u003c/em\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eReads the common containers draw.io writes — \u003ccode\u003e.drawio\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.drawio.xml\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.drawio.png\u003c/code\u003e (embedded diagram), and \u003ccode\u003e.drawio.svg\u003c/code\u003e — including compressed payloads that look like base64 garbage in an editor.\nFor Mermaid, it accepts \u003ccode\u003e.mmd\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.mermaid\u003c/code\u003e, and one or more fenced \u003ccode\u003emermaid\u003c/code\u003e blocks in Markdown.\nFor Excalidraw, it accepts \u003ccode\u003e.excalidraw\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e.excalidraw.json\u003c/code\u003e scene files (not \u003ccode\u003e.excalidraw.png\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e.excalidraw.svg\u003c/code\u003e exports). It parses text only: no rendering, JavaScript, browser, network, or followed click targets.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe four dials\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-the-four-dials\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: The four dials\" href=\"#the-four-dials\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe point isn't conversion, it's \u003cstrong\u003efitting the output to where it's going\u003c/strong\u003e. Same source file, three different diagrams:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eDial\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eOptions\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhat it changes\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFormat\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehtml\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003esvg\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003epng\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003ehtml+png\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe deliverable. SVG for Figma, PNG for slides, HTML for the web.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSize\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003edoc-inline\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003edoc-wide\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eslide-16x9\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eslide-4x3\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003esocial-og\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003esocial-square\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eprint-a4-landscape\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eprint-a3-landscape\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eprint-letter-landscape\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003efit\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003eviewBox\u003c/code\u003e \u003cstrong\u003eand the type ramp\u003c/strong\u003e — a projected slide gets 16px node names, not 12px.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDetail\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efaithful\u003c/code\u003e (≤24 nodes, zoned) · \u003ccode\u003ebalanced\u003c/code\u003e (≤12) · \u003ccode\u003esimplified\u003c/code\u003e (≤7)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHow much of the source survives, via a fixed degrade ladder — decorations, then duplicates, then leaf clusters, then infrastructure.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAudience\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eengineer\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003emixed\u003c/code\u003e · \u003ccode\u003eexecutive\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe \u003cem\u003ewording\u003c/em\u003e, not the count. \u003ccode\u003eAuth Service / JWT · RS256 · :8443\u003c/code\u003e → \u003ccode\u003eAuth Service / token check\u003c/code\u003e → \u003ccode\u003eSign-in\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery import ends with a \u003cstrong\u003efidelity ledger\u003c/strong\u003e — what got merged, collapsed, or dropped. You know the source; you'd notice anyway.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Detail: balanced · 12 source nodes → 8 drawn\nCollapsed: \u0026quot;Token valid?\u0026quot; decision → edge label on Gateway → Auth\nDropped:   1 sticky note (\u0026quot;legacy path, to be retired\u0026quot;) — unconnected in source\nKept in full: the request path (Web/Mobile → Gateway → Orders → Postgres)\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eDetail: balanced · 12 source nodes → 8 drawn\nCollapsed: \"Token valid?\" decision → edge label on Gateway → Auth\nDropped:   1 sticky note (\"legacy path, to be retired\") — unconnected in source\nKept in full: the request path (Web/Mobile → Gateway → Orders → Postgres)\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat never carries over: source or renderer coordinates, source palette, source fonts, draw.io's diagonal connector spaghetti, Mermaid's automatic layout, or Excalidraw's hand-drawn geometry. What always does: components, relationships, grouping, and direction. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/import-drawio.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-drawio.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/import-mermaid.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-mermaid.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/import-excalidraw.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-excalidraw.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, and \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/output-spec.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ereferences/output-spec.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv or social cards. path/to/diagram.html --registry\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr just ask in natural language:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026quot;Export this diagram as SVG and PNG.\u0026quot;\n\u0026quot;Save my-diagram.html as PNG.\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e\"Export this diagram as SVG and PNG.\"\n\"Save my-diagram.html as PNG.\"\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSVG\u003c/strong\u003e — extracts the \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;svg\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e node and injects Google Fonts so it renders standalone in browsers, Figma, and Illustrator.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePNG\u003c/strong\u003e — rasterizes the diagram via Playwright at 2× by default. One-time setup: \u003ccode\u003epip install playwright \u0026amp;\u0026amp; playwright install chromium\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBoth formats are diagram-only — editorial cards and headers from \u003ccode\u003e-full\u003c/code\u003e variants aren't included. For a screenshot of the full editorial layout, use your browser's print-to-PDF or full-page screenshot. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/export.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/export.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e for the full procedure.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--registry\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — for diagrams using the \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/semantic-patterns.md\"\u003etraceable block decomposition\u003c/a\u003e pattern, also emits \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;basename\u0026gt;.registry.json\u003c/code\u003e, a structured projection of every block's \u003ccode\u003edata-block-*\u003c/code\u003e metadata. Combine with either raster format or run alone. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/export-registry.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/export-registry.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor motion-enabled HTML, export the explicit final state: open \u003ccode\u003e?motion=static\u003c/code\u003e, wait for \u003ccode\u003edocument.fonts.ready\u003c/code\u003e, and confirm the motion root has \u003ccode\u003edata-frame=\"static\"\u003c/code\u003e before capture. Use \u003ccode\u003e?motion=step\u0026amp;step=N\u003c/code\u003e only when a named intermediate frame was requested.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eArchitecture\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-architecture\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Architecture\" href=\"#architecture\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eProgressive disclosure. \u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e routes behavior first when needed, then layout. Semantic, type, and animation references load only when relevant.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" + marketplace package gate\n│   ├── test-plugin-package.py       — adversarial package-gate tests\n│   ├── lint-render.py               — Chromium rendered-layout checker\n│   ├── verify-doctor.py test-verify-docs-sync.py     — docs/routing-surface gate tests\n│   └── fixtures/\n│       ├── sample-flowchart.mmd\n│       ├── sample-readme-with-mermaid.md\n│       ├── sample-adversarial.mmd\n│       ├── sample-whiteboard.excalidraw\n│       └── sample-adversarial.excalidraw\n├── docs/cookbook.md                 — operator recipes for editable installs and common tasks\n├── docs/adr/ WebP previews the README renders\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis keeps the agent's working context tight: routine diagrams load one type reference; behavior-rich diagrams add the routed semantic reference; animation adds its contract only when selected.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContributing / skin lint\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-contributing--skin-lint\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Contributing / skin lint\" href=\"#contributing--skin-lint\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBefore submitting a new example, run \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/lint-skin.py \u0026lt;your-new-example.html\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e.\nThe repository-wide check \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/lint-skin.py --all --baseline\u003c/code\u003e covers examples and templates and must stay green.\nCI separately verifies semantic routing, animated-example structure, animated skin, every shipped motion asset, and adversarial mutations of the controller contract, reporting later gate outcomes even when an earlier gate fails. Semantic routing must pass \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-semantic-motion.py --markdown-only\u003c/code\u003e; the animated example has a separate \u003ccode\u003e--example-only\u003c/code\u003e gate. Every shipped motion template/example must also pass \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-motion.py --shipped\u003c/code\u003e.\nThe linter's \u003ccode\u003ea11y\u003c/code\u003e category rejects diagram SVGs without a resolving accessible name,\nan empty or misplaced title/description, or unsafe bare \u003ccode\u003etitle\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003edesc\u003c/code\u003e IDs. It also pins the exact reviewed motion controller and rejects remote assets, CSS \u003ccode\u003e@import\u003c/code\u003e, non-fragment CSS \u003ccode\u003eurl()\u003c/code\u003e, and executable attributes such as \u003ccode\u003eonclick\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003esrcdoc\u003c/code\u003e.\nIf you touch the draw.io import path, \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-drawio-import.py\u003c/code\u003e must also pass —\nit drives the real extractor against \u003ccode\u003escripts/fixtures/sample-architecture.drawio\u003c/code\u003e in all four\ncontainer formats and checks the references stay in sync.\nIf you touch the Mermaid import path, \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-mermaid-import.py\u003c/code\u003e must also pass —\nit covers all supported grammars, multi-block Markdown, adversarial labels, trust-boundary\nbehavior, resource caps, named failures, and reference/command wiring.\nIf you touch the Excalidraw import path, \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-excalidraw-import.py\u003c/code\u003e must also\npass — it covers scene parsing, bound labels, groups and frames, adversarial labels,\ntrust-boundary behavior, resource caps, named failures, and reference/command wiring.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLabel placement is gated geometrically: \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-geometry.py --all\u003c/code\u003e fails CI when a label mask overlaps a node declared later in the document, because the node fill would clip the text at render time. \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-verify-geometry.py\u003c/code\u003e keeps that checker honest in both directions.\nDiagrams using the traceable block decomposition pattern get a structural gate on top of that: \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-block-registry.py --all\u003c/code\u003e fails CI on a duplicate \u003ccode\u003edata-block-id\u003c/code\u003e, a \u003ccode\u003edata-block-parent\u003c/code\u003e that doesn't resolve to another block in the same file, a cycle in the parent chain, a blank \u003ccode\u003edata-block-id\u003c/code\u003e, or a missing or blank \u003ccode\u003edata-block-name\u003c/code\u003e — the same defects that would make \u003ccode\u003e--registry\u003c/code\u003e's exported JSON (see \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/export-registry.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eexport-registry.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e) misrepresent the tree it claims to describe. \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-verify-block-registry.py\u003c/code\u003e keeps that checker honest in both directions.\nTreemaps get a second geometric gate, because their whole claim is that area \u003cem\u003eis\u003c/em\u003e the encoding: \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-treemap.py --all\u003c/code\u003e fails CI when a cell's share of the drawn area doesn't match the value printed inside it, or when a label overruns the cell it names. It measures area error as a \u003cem\u003erelative\u003c/em\u003e figure — an absolute one passes exactly the small cells most likely to be wrong. \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-verify-treemap.py\u003c/code\u003e keeps it honest in both directions.\nWaterfalls get the same treatment, because their whole claim is that the running total is conserved: \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-waterfall.py --all\u003c/code\u003e fails CI when the declared start, deltas, and end don't reconcile, when a bridge bar is drawn anywhere other than its two running levels on the shared scale, when a carry connector is missing or sits at the wrong level, when a delta prints without an explicit sign, or when the two directions collapse into one fill. \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-verify-waterfall.py\u003c/code\u003e keeps it honest in both directions.\nDocs and routing surfaces are themselves gated: \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/verify-docs-sync.py\u003c/code\u003e fails CI if the SKILL.md description loses a type's lexical hook, the gallery can't reach a shipped example, the README tree names a file that doesn't exist, a relative reference link is broken, a scanner-visible support path is not shipped inside the skill package, or any command/prompt surface drifts from its routed reference. \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-verify-docs-sync.py\u003c/code\u003e exercises those newer checks adversarially, including the strict-bundler behavior used by Hermes Agent. The skill also ships \u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/scripts/self_check.py\u003c/code\u003e — a distilled output checker installed agents can run on their own generated diagrams; \u003ccode\u003epython3 scripts/test-self-check.py\u003c/code\u003e keeps it honest. Settled design decisions (why one pinned controller, why patterns never add types, the autoplay policy, the SKILL.md byte cap, why label placement is verified geometrically, and why client profiles use marker-first resolution) live as short ADRs in \u003ccode\u003edocs/adr/\u003c/code\u003e — read them before relitigating one, add one when you settle a new policy.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll pull requests and pushes are automatically validated across Linux, Windows, and macOS runners via GitHub Actions CI (\u003ccode\u003e.github/workflows/ci.yml\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003elint-skin.py\u003c/code\u003e reads the source. \u003ccode\u003elint-render.py\u003c/code\u003e renders it — headless Chromium\nreports what actually got painted, which catches content cut off by the SVG\nviewport, collapsed SVGs, horizontal page overflow, missing local assets and JS\nerrors. Both run in CI on every pull request.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"pip install playwright \u0026amp;\u0026amp; playwright install chromium   # same dep as PNG export\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --self-test              # checks the checks\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --all                   # examples and templates\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py \u0026lt;your-new-example.html\u0026gt;\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --fonts --all           # measure with the real webfonts\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epip install playwright \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026amp;\u0026amp;\u003c/span\u003e playwright install chromium   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e same dep as PNG export\u003c/span\u003e\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --self-test              \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e checks the checks\u003c/span\u003e\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --all                   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e examples and templates\u003c/span\u003e\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eyour-new-example.html\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\npython3 scripts/lint-render.py --fonts --all           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e measure with the real webfonts\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClipping is measured by paint, not geometry: \u003ccode\u003egetBoundingClientRect()\u003c/code\u003e on an SVG\nchild ignores stroke width, markers and filter bleed, and knows nothing about\n\u003ccode\u003eclip-path\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003eoverflow: visible\u003c/code\u003e, so it both misses real clipping and invents\nclipping that isn't there. Instead each SVG is screenshot as authored and again\nwith its \u003ccode\u003eoverflow\u003c/code\u003e released, and the two are diffed — ink that appears outside\nwas being cut off. Releases are staged — the SVG alone, then each clipping\nancestor — so a wrapper release can't mask spill at the SVG's own edge, and an SVG\nauthored \u003ccode\u003eoverflow: visible\u003c/code\u003e inside a clipping wrapper is still checked.\n\u003ccode\u003e--self-test\u003c/code\u003e asserts all of that on 23 cases, over half of them cases that must\n\u003cem\u003enot\u003c/em\u003e be flagged, and it also asserts the DOM is byte-identical after measuring.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNo golden images, so there is nothing to re-record and no PNGs in the repo.\nNetwork is cut at the browser's resolver, which covers WebSockets and anything\nelse that bypasses request routing, with request routing as a second layer;\n\u003ccode\u003e--fonts\u003c/code\u003e excludes exactly the two Google Fonts hostnames and allows them only\nover HTTPS. Since the oracle is pixels, CI pins Playwright and its Chromium build\nrather than installing whatever is newest.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFont metrics differ between the default run and \u003ccode\u003e--fonts\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/strong\u003e With network\nblocked — the default, and what CI runs — text is laid out in the fallback faces,\nnot Instrument Serif and Geist. That is deterministic and machine-independent,\nwhich is what a linter needs, but it is not what your reader sees. Run\n\u003ccode\u003e--fonts --all\u003c/code\u003e locally when you care whether real text fits its box.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat loads when\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-loads-when\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What loads when\" href=\"#what-loads-when\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAt startup, the agent sees only the skill name and description. When a request matches, it loads \u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e; semantic, type, and animation references are pulled in only when relevant.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eYou ask for…\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eAgent loads\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Make me a flowchart\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/type-flowchart.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Build an architecture diagram\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/type-architecture.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Show what was added, removed, changed, moved, or rewired in this migration\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/type-architecture-delta.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Compare why these two policy requests differ\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/semantic-patterns.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/type-flowchart.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Animate that policy trace\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePrior selection + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/animation.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Onboard this skill to my site\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/onboarding.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/style-guide.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Use my saved Acme client profile\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/profiles.md\u003c/code\u003e + diagram\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + version\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/primitive-sketchy.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Give me a terminal / CLI-window version\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/primitive-terminal.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Redraw this .drawio file for my deck\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-drawio.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/output-spec.md\u003c/code\u003e + the chosen type's reference\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Redraw this Mermaid block for my deck\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-mermaid.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/output-spec.md\u003c/code\u003e + the chosen type's reference\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"Redraw this Excalidraw sketch for my deck\"\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/import-excalidraw.md\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003ereferences/output-spec.md\u003c/code\u003e + the chosen type's reference\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRoutine static diagram-making (any visual type)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e + that one type's reference, plus \u003ccode\u003ereferences/primitives-core.md\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003ereferences/layout-budget.md\u003c/code\u003e only when it needs exact markup or a per-type budget row\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNo matter how many types exist, the agent only reads the one you need. Add a new type tomorrow and nothing else changes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt's working if…\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-its-working-if\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: It's working if…\" href=\"#its-working-if\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA routine request (\"make me a flowchart\") loads \u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e, exactly one type reference, and at most the two core references (\u003ccode\u003eprimitives-core.md\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elayout-budget.md\u003c/code\u003e), and nothing else.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore drawing, the agent states the chosen type, pattern, size, and planned cuts, then renders.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe output is one \u003ccode\u003e.html\u003c/code\u003e file that opens double-clicked, offline, with no network requests beyond Google Fonts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eScreen readers announce the diagram's title and description; \u003ccode\u003eprefers-reduced-motion\u003c/code\u003e shows the complete static frame.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epython3 skills/diagram-design/scripts/self_check.py \u0026lt;file\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e prints \u003ccode\u003eOK\u003c/code\u003e on the generated file.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAfter brand onboarding, new diagrams use your site's paper, ink, accent, and fonts — with a fidelity receipt naming each.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf any of these fail, that's a bug worth filing.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe design system (in one paragraph)\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-the-design-system-in-one-paragraph\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: The design system (in one paragraph)\" href=\"#the-design-system-in-one-paragraph\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOne accent color, 1–2 focal elements per diagram. Three font families: Instrument Serif (title + italic callouts), Geist sans (node names), Geist Mono (technical sublabels). 1px hairline borders, no shadows, max border-radius 10px. Every coord, width, and gap divisible by 4 — non-negotiable, it's what keeps the diagrams from feeling AI-generated. Mono is for technical content (ports, URLs, field types), not a blanket \"dev\" aesthetic. Coral-tinted focal nodes draw the eye to the 1–2 things that matter. Full spec in \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/SKILL.md#5-design-system\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrimitives\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-primitives\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Primitives\" href=\"#primitives\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAnnotation callout\u003c/strong\u003e — italic Instrument Serif + dashed Bézier leader, for editorial asides that sit in the margins. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/primitive-annotation.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/primitive-annotation.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSketchy filter\u003c/strong\u003e — SVG turbulence + displacement map for a hand-drawn variant. Good for essays, not for technical docs. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/primitive-sketchy.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/primitive-sketchy.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIcon set\u003c/strong\u003e — 87 monochrome IT/cloud icons (laptop, phone, user, server, database, Docker, Kubernetes, AWS, Azure, GitHub, Postgres…) for richer architecture and sequence diagrams. Stroked icons from \u003ca href=\"https://tabler.io/icons\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eTabler Icons\u003c/a\u003e (MIT); brand silhouettes from \u003ca href=\"https://simpleicons.org\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eSimple Icons\u003c/a\u003e (CC0). Each icon uses \u003ccode\u003ecurrentColor\u003c/code\u003e so it inherits the editorial skin or your onboarded brand. See \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/references/primitive-icons.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eskills/diagram-design/references/primitive-icons.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e; browse the \u003ca href=\"/cathrynlavery/diagram-design/blob/main/skills/diagram-design/assets/icons.html\"\u003egallery\u003c/a\u003e. Regenerate with \u003ccode\u003epython scripts/build-icons.py\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen \u003cem\u003enot\u003c/em\u003e to use this skill\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-when-not-to-use-this-skill\" 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuick unicode diagrams\u003c/strong\u003e for tweets or terminal output → wiretext-style skill.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLists of anything\u003c/strong\u003e → a table or bullets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBefore/after comparisons\u003c/strong\u003e → a table.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOne-shape \"diagrams\"\u003c/strong\u003e — a single box with a label → just write the sentence.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBefore drawing, ask: \u003cem\u003ewould a reader learn more from this than from a well-written paragraph?\u003c/em\u003e If no, don't draw.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2
